Yukino: "It's what the American president said. It's a nice phrase."
*...Yeah, of course her memories haven't come back.*
*Qoo's final job was perfect. The slate was wiped clean.*
Yukino: "Pu-chin. To change the law, you pretty much have to become the Prime Minister, right?"
Irina: "Well, that's the fastest route."
Yukino: "Then, I'll become the Prime Minister!"
Junichiro: "Yukino... Are you serious!?"
Yukino: "Ahaha. Well, the monogamy law is one thing, but I think there's a lot of things that aren't right in Nippon. And there's a lot of people who agree."
Yukino: "I'll keep help from those people, and together we'll change this country!"
*The flames of passion are lit in her eyes. When she gets like this, Yukino was really strong.*
*I think back to the time she became President.*
Junichiro: "You're going to have to study a whole lot, you know?"
Yukino: "I'll give it my all!"
Junichiro: "You'll have to give a ton of speeches, you know?"
Yukino: "...It's a little embarrassing, but if Jun-kun and Pu-chin give me a pat on the head, I think I can do it!"
Junichiro: "You little..."
Irina: "Pff, haha."
*And now the ever-shy Yukino is saying she won't shrink from speeches. Her desire seems to be the real thing.*
*But Yukino really is mysterious. Seeing her put her all into something makes me want to cheer her on. She makes me feel like she can definitely do it.*
Junichiro: "...You're serious, right?"
Yukino: "Yep!"
Irina: "Yeah, that's the spirit! As expected of my comrade! I'll put my all into helping you! Together let's change Nippon. No, let's change the world!"
Irina: "I want to make this planet into a peaceful and quiet planet."
Yukino: "I think that's a good thing... but also a really hard thing."
Irina: "You're right. The road to peace is long. Religion, race, wealth, history. There are many reasons people discriminate against each other. The price has to be paid in blood sometimes."
Irina: "But, you know, history has been going for thousands of years. I think it's about time for it to take the next step."
Irina: "World government... Not communism, not democracy, but a whole new type of world. We, no, our generation will make it."
Yukino: "Hueeee..."
Junichiro: "Your dream is enormous, Irina."
Yukino: "Yeah, it's not just your breasts."
Irina: "That was a few words too many."
Irina & Yukino & Junichiro: "Pff, ahahaha!"
*For a while we just laugh and laugh. Then I looked up at the blue sky and a thought fills my head.*
*Hey, Qoo.*
*The day we meet you guys again might not be so far away after all.*
